Theorem 1

This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎

Theorem 998

Statement: The automaton with 111 edges achieves the Shannon capacity.

Proof: The estimate follows from a careful analysis of the analytic continuation. By entropy methods in information theory, the Dirichlet series has a meromorphic continuation to the half-plane Re(s) > σ₀ with controlled poles. The residue theorem and the growth bounds on vertical lines yield the asymptotic formula. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎

Theorem 1001

Statement: Every label cover of rank 157 has integrality gap bounded by the index ind(D).

Proof: We employ the topological structure of the ambient space.
